5:Decision: `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail`. Codex Architect applies the owner's full-v69-menu choice as a narrow amendment to the 2026-09-13 navigation rule and `D-2026-10-02-grammar-ownership-test`. The operator rail shows all eleven roots in six v69 groups, headed `OPERATIONS`, `INTELLIGENCE`, `WORK`, `ASSURANCE`, `BUSINESS`, `ADMIN`; only Sites (`/sites`) and Findings (`/findings`) are live W1 links. Other roots are visibly and accessibly `Not available yet` text with no destination or keyboard action; site-scoped pages do not make a cross-site root live. The Simulator Lab workspace-footer link appears only with its registered gated route, and unsupported demo-workspace/count copy is omitted. The navigation guard and route/component tests must prove the full inventory, absent destinations, real live routes, gate behavior, and 390 px access while retaining the separate Site tab/subtab bans. Exact contract: `.agent/architect-W1-UX-07-answers.md`. This decision does not promote panels or record owner acceptance.
7:Decision: `D-2026-10-17-w1-ux-06`. Codex Architect accepts W1-UX-06 questions 1-2: the S1 `READY_TO_REPORT` badge reads `Set up to report` with its configured tone and configuration-only meaning, and the Readiness strip reads `Set-up steps` because it has no contact read. Accept question 3 for current server contact with trusted, known old reported facts: the selected gateway state line says `In contact; its latest report is old`; unknown or untrusted fact age remains explicitly unconfirmed, and neither state claims equipment health. Amend question 4: unrecognised Readiness and Health codes get non-positive headlines, and zero enabled gateways never produce `All 0 enabled gateways`; preserve the recognised no-source reason and keep readiness-dependent steps from appearing complete on an unrecognised readiness code alone. Exact words, conditions and test expectations are in `.agent/architect-W1-UX-06-answers.md`. This refines `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`, `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health` and `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`; it neither promotes a panel nor records owner acceptance.
13:Decision: `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux-disclosure`. Codex Architect accepts the W1-UX-01 narrow disclosure scan as an amendment to `D-2026-10-04-plain-language-analysis-views`, with the exact banned disclosure-prose terms, permitted individually labelled references, and case-sensitive raw-code detection defined in `.agent/architect-W1-ux-disclosure-answers.md`. The closed disclosure is scanned, never exempted as a container; the existing single-value exemption rule stays. Raw IDs, versions, digests and reasons may appear only as labelled exact values, never in the collapsed summary. Raw-code matching does not flag ordinary units or acronyms including SOC, UTC, kW, kWh and PV. Plain state, material limits, scope and as-of time stay visible outside the disclosure. This does not record owner acceptance or change the W1 walkthrough gate.

4165:Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s405-stored-demo-provenance`. Under the owner's 2026-10-15 absence delegation, accept S4-05 Q1-Q3 as amended by Follow-up (plan review M1) in `.agent/architect-S4-05-answers.md`. A server-authorized, immutable pre-admission publication binds an exact Batch V2 commitment to tenant/Site, gateway/source, external batch ID and canonical digest. `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` additionally binds fixture/spec ID and version, including authored P01 and P02. `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` binds an ordinary simulator batch to a server-recorded run identity; `SIMULATED` mode or a private manifest alone is insufficient. Publication and admission serialize on the scoped batch key; post-admission registration and mismatched commitments fail. Accepted IDs inherit immutable provenance, duplicates retain their original accepted ID, and historical unbound IDs stay unknown. Finding and consequence source state derives from each revision's exact accepted IDs, including consequence power intervals. Coherent all-authored or all-registered-simulation sets may show a current amount only when LR eligibility, exact basis, currentness, source-open and quantity/price gates also pass. Mixed or unknown source blocks a current amount in Finding Financials, consequence and S5 Overview regardless of `CURRENT PRICED` or LR eligibility; prior values remain audit-only. Real data remains unknown and unable to show current money until a separate server-authorized `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` follow-on is implemented. Create open registry node `I-attested-real-source-provenance` in `real_pilot_requires` before resolving `I-stored-demo-provenance` or installing a pilot timer; S4-05 may resolve before the real-source node, but its resolution is not pilot readiness. Amend S5-01's source gate to consume stored exact-revision provenance whichever slice merges second. Extend the existing PostgreSQL evidence-ledger writer, not the writer count: rule 8 permits only its INSERTs to publication/link rows, refuses other writers and mutations, preserves existing writer protections, and is backed by database immutability. Visible copy and pilot designation still require the owner's W1 checkpoint; this decision grants neither owner approval nor W1/pilot readiness.
4188:Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s405b-label`. For S4-05b, use interim operator labels `Prepared example data` for exact authored evidence, `Generated test data` for exact registered generated evidence, `Sources do not agree` for mixed evidence, and `Source unverified` for unknown evidence, each with adjacent plain source-limit text. Positive labels explicitly say the records are not readings from physical equipment; mixed/unknown never imply a physical source or zero cost. Keep exact source IDs and basis in deeper detail, with no vocabulary-scan exemption for source-state prose. A partly sealed registered Lab stage refuses the entire Commit with `SOURCE_PUBLICATION_REFUSED`; no sealed subset is released. Phase 2 also retires `demonstration_label.py` from Action reads and resolves each Action's pinned origin FindingRevision, failing closed to unknown when unresolved. Full ruling: `.agent/architect-S4-05b-answers.md`. Final visible wording and placement remain the owner's W1 copy checkpoint before S4-05b merge.

frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:21:Start with **one mini-grid Site and one assessed window**. Use the existing causal simulator as the S2–S7 producer. A physical protocol capture or labelled vendor-format fixture becomes a contract peer at the first real pilot, once an input is available. Retain the static v6.9 mock screens as interaction/content references; a slice implements the supported portion with honest missing states, not a full static screen filled with invented data. Existing JSON/YAML data stays available for migration comparison, but new product writes use the target stores. The Site shell can keep its current routes and reusable components where they help.
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:27:| **S1 · Foundation:** `foundation`, `readiness`, and the configured portion of `sld` | Postgres Tenant/Site/Asset/Gateway/Source registration; immutable valid-time Foundation and SignalBinding revisions; one mini-grid role set and authenticated gateway snapshot download. `GET /api/sites/{id}/foundation?at=` returns the version, topology, bindings and explicit configuration gaps. | Create or import Kobo, inspect device-to-asset/role mappings, change a reserve/control or binding with a new effective version, and reopen an earlier time to see its prior basis. Readiness reports unbound required roles; SLD uses actual registered topology. No live operating status is implied by configured equipment. |
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:28:| **S2 · Evidence:** `evidence`, `evidence-analysis`, and Lab `gateway-status`/Commit detail | S2-01a reads the registered Foundation/binding basis into Lab runs; S2-01b gives simulated reports a separate SQLite gateway, V3 identity and sealed Batch V2 outbox; S2-01c publishes diagnostics and fault scenarios. S2-02 adds authenticated HTTPS, Postgres accepted/rejected/duplicate decisions, central diagnostics and Evidence queries. The physical protocol adapter is a first-real-pilot gate. | Generate a registered Kobo simulated run, Stage then Commit; disconnect/reconnect the gateway and inspect queue depth, upload result, accepted/delayed/rejected records and central freshness after reload. Replay identical bytes to get duplicates without extra facts. S2 does not claim captured physical-device equivalence. Legacy demo commits remain comparable through migration, not copied as private truth. |
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:32:| **S6 · Work / Action / Verification:** Site `changes`, `recommendation`, `verification` and the Actions portion of `sitework` | Persist Action, ActionEvent, VerificationPlanRevision and VerificationOutcome; immutable policy-change artifact/event linked to Site Foundation; later-window analytic comparison with target and service/reserve guardrails. Site work read model joins the same Finding and Action identities. | Accept a recommendation, publish one controlled reserve-SOC Foundation revision in S6-01, then run Lab under that basis and Commit later gateway evidence in S6-02; inspect activity separately from outcome. Show Verified, Ineffective or Inconclusive from target **and** guardrails; opening either window resolves the exact evidence and plan revision. A later recurrence can reopen the issue. No global Actions index yet. |

If the simulator cannot produce a causal battery stress observation, show battery state and replacement exposure as Unknown/withheld. No captured-device fallback exists in the S2 demo source choice. No remaining-life date or failure diagnosis without supporting input. |

frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:58:Every packet names **one visible change to a mock surface**, the exact persisted object/read API that makes it real, a runnable demo path, a missing/conflicting-evidence state, and an evidence-lineage check. Tasks may carry migrations, adapters, API and UI together. Do not create isolated “Postgres schema”, “gateway framework”, “analytics framework” or “tab shell” tasks whose only visible result is infrastructure. If a prerequisite needs its own task, its closeout must still change a specific inspectable Site or Lab state and name the next screen it unlocks.
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:64:This is a cross-cutting dependency stream alongside the Site screens. It owns source production and delivery, never product claims. The Architect has set S2-01a/b/c as the reviewed cuts and retained S2-02 as the evidence owner checkpoint. From S1-01 until S2-01a closes, the imported Kobo Lab Draft/run/Commit path is deliberately blocked; unimported legacy YAML Sites remain available under their existing boundary. The short S2-01a cut restores registered Kobo Lab runs, while Commit remains blocked until S2-02.

frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79:**Physical peer source in effect:** S2 uses simulated reports only. At the first real pilot, choose one real-device capture if available, or one labelled synthetic fixture built from vendor protocol documentation; prove protocol frames, register scaling, units and device clocks before claiming real-adapter equivalence. That future gate replaces the simulator batch while preserving the backend, analytic and UI contracts. It is not an S2 acceptance check. The S7 demo cannot use captured-device replay as a battery fallback under this source choice.
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:84:- **Typed analytic boundary:** `EvidenceQuery` returns typed normalized readings/records with quality, time, asset/role, configuration basis and accepted-evidence IDs. The dispatch analytic consumes this plus a typed public configuration view. SQL rows, `jsonb`, canonical envelope text, Lab world records and source adapters stop at repository/normalization boundaries. Contract tests compare simulated and captured real inputs after this boundary.
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:86:- **Domain algorithms:** dispatch thresholds, cold-chain excursion detection and EV session logic are domain-design work. The platform plan requires their inputs, revision/lineage outputs and claim limits, not more universal framework or algorithm prose. S1–S7 cover one mini-grid Site; later domains add role sets and analytics through the same seam.
frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:91:After S4, a **Findings index** can already list real Finding objects, but build the global root only when the Site detail and lifecycle are useful. After S6, **Actions** and **Sites** become filtering/indexing over durable Site/Action summaries; **Portfolio** aggregates comparable consequences and priorities with currency/window/coverage boundaries; **Evidence** aggregates Site capability/readiness; **Assets** indexes Site Asset/Health/work records after S7. These are later product roots, not prerequisites or empty screens for the Site spine. Cold-chain and EV are later Site-domain passes using the same ingestion, revision, work and shell contracts; neither should be built by copying mini-grid screens with different labels.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:7:The [v6.9 interactive mock screens](../../mockups/v69/index.html) ask one question across Portfolio, Site, Performance, Findings, Evidence, Financials, Assets and Work: **what happened, what did it cost or put at risk, what should we do, and what evidence supports that answer?** AssetOps must connect physical operation to cost, revenue, reliability, maintenance burden and asset-life risk without turning missing evidence into false certainty. The Lab demonstrates the source of observations; it is not the source of product conclusions.

frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:27:**Invariant:** replace the simulated gateway with a real gateway that emits equivalent `SourceEnvelope`s and the ingestion, analytics, read models and operator UI remain unchanged. Only explicit source provenance and the Lab entry point differ. At the first real pilot, a contract test must run a real capture or labelled vendor-format fixture through the physical path and compare normalized envelopes and downstream claims with the simulated path. The S2 demo proves the simulated path only.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:33:| One Python/FastAPI modular monolith, React operator UI, Postgres product database. Separate gateway process and gated Lab process. | Current stack already proves the journey; one transactional backend is fast to build and inspect. Separate processes enforce the source boundary. | Microservices, Kubernetes, generic event platform. |
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:37:| Small domain seam: `site_type` + versioned semantic role bindings + one analytic/read-model module per domain. | Mini-grid, cold-chain and EV share workflow but need different proof and language. | Plugin discovery, user-authored analytic DSL, universal asset ontology. |
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:71:The world owns physics, controls, scenario causes and oracle expectations; simulated devices own only what their real counterparts could report. The simulated gateway cannot read private world state except through device reports. Its SQLite store persists staged envelopes and upload state exactly as a physical gateway would. Preserve Lab Draft/Stage/Commit only as a **release gate**: Draft may execute and inspect private truth; Stage seals public envelopes; Commit authorizes delivery through the same batch API and records a manifest/receipt. Commit cannot write evidence, Findings or site projections. Private truth, controller intent, causes, oracle and run controls live in a separate Lab store and Lab-only API with access controls; no foreign key or import from normal analytics to those objects. Simulated provenance may label a claim, never alter its calculation.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:75:The shared shell owns identity, site/window selection, evidence drawer, claim status, Findings, money and work navigation. A small registry keyed by `site_type` selects explicit evidence roles, analytic functions, summary sections and labels. A role binding maps physical signals to analytic inputs for a *valid period*; it is not a runtime plugin. Missing roles lower or block claim capability. Mini-grid roles include generator state/power, feeder demand, PV availability, battery reserve/SOC, fuel and service baseline; cold-chain roles include chamber temperature, setpoint/policy, door/defrost, compressor and backup state; EV roles include session request/energy/end state, connector availability, charger limits and tariff. Keep roles minimal per analytic, not one global schema that pretends domains are interchangeable.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:80:| Site Overview `GET /sites/{id}/overview` | Site objective cards, bounded metrics, timeline, latest Finding/consequence/action, source freshness | Mini-grid service/dispatch; cold temperature/backup; EV sessions/availability. |
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:84:| Financials `GET /sites/{id}/financials`, `/financials` | Consequence revisions, assumption versions and compatible rollups | Fuel/maintenance, product/reliability exposure, EV revenue/service loss; show unpriced risk. |

frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:83:| `envelope_outbox` | PK `message_id`; `gateway_id`, `source_id`, `sequence_epoch`, `sequence`, `source_time`, `envelope_text`, `sha256`, `state`, `created_at`, `last_attempt_at?`; `msg42 / seq42 / PENDING` | `UNIQUE(source_id,sequence_epoch,sequence)`; append bytes once; state `STAGED,READY,IN_FLIGHT,ACKED,DEAD_LETTER`. frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131:The existing v1/v2 envelope and v1 batch parser remain read-compatible for stored demo history. V3 **changes identity semantics**: `message_id = SHA256(length-delimited(site_id,gateway_id,source_id,sequence_epoch,sequence))`, with the tuple durably allocated once. Exact byte framing and test vectors are part of the contract task; do not concatenate without separators or trust a sender-supplied digest. Sequence scope is `(gateway,source,epoch)`; monotonic arrival is not required. `published_at` is when gateway seals publication, not a second measurement time. Source clock uncertainty is expressed as quality/diagnostic metadata, never correction of `source_time`. Batch V2 is homogeneous in source/mapping/Foundation reference; each envelope is still checked against effective configuration at its own time. The batch hash is over canonical bytes excluding the `payload_sha256` field; the receipt hash similarly excludes `receipt_sha256`. Batch `source_mode` is compared with authenticated gateway registration and is only provenance after that check. Wire IDs for site/gateway/source resolve registered UUID rows; device/signal IDs remain scoped source strings. A production batch must not carry Lab-private cause or truth; keep any Lab run reference in a private manifest keyed by batch ID.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:173:`QueryScope.window` is half-open `[start,end)` and required for analytic/product queries. `knowledge_at` makes historical audits reproducible; normal UI defaults to current server time. `selected_revision_id` freezes a drill-down even if the surrounding window changes. `AnalyticInputSet.input_sha256` hashes sorted evidence IDs **and their content digests**, resolved configuration/binding revisions, window, analytic/model version and canonical role coverage. The financial consequence has a separate basis digest including its assumption revisions. Missing required roles produce a bounded Unknown/Indeterminate assessment with explicit reason; the analytic never reads world state or a Lab run ID. `FinancialConsequence` has no amount when the technical quantity is supported but price/demand is not. `Measured`, `Modelled`, `Extrapolated` and `Scenario` are distinct bases; UI must expose them.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:183:**Initial API contract:** `POST /api/ingestion/batches` returns terminal `200` receipt or `202 IN_PROGRESS` receipt; `GET /api/ingestion/batches/{id}` returns the same durable state. `409` is reserved for same batch ID/different bytes or registered identity conflict, `422` for malformed contract, `401/403` for authentication/authorization. Normal reads use `GET /api/sites/{id}/evidence`, `/evidence/records/{id}`, `/overview`, `/performance/{view}`, `/findings/{id}` with `from`, `to` and optional `revision_id`; list reads paginate by stable `(source_time,id)` or `(created_at,id)` cursor. All responses include scope/window, revision IDs, evidence coverage and projection watermark. The UI treats a lagging projection as pending, not healthy. Lab Commit calls this same HTTP boundary with a registered simulated gateway; no privileged product-write endpoint exists.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:187:**Demo-to-Postgres cutover:** create Tenant/Site/Gateway/Source registrations from existing Site records, import immutable Foundation and mapping versions, then replay sealed committed batch bytes through the new ingestion service using the legacy parsers. Preserve original batch/message IDs, envelope text, source times and mode; assign new database UUIDs only to internal rows. Compare accepted/rejected/conflict counts and representative evidence/dispatch claims against the file-backed baseline. Keep `var/sites`, Lab runs, stages and committed artifacts untouched and read-only during verification. Switch product reads to Postgres only after the comparison passes; new Lab Commit posts the V2 batch/receipt path. Do not import private world trajectories into the product database. An existing batch whose required public configuration cannot be registered is reported as a migration exception, never guessed into a new Foundation version.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:199:| Asset registration and warranty | Existing `asset.id` remains physical identity; `asset_registration_revision` records audited name/spec/vendor/serial/location changes with valid and recorded time where claim relevant. `warranty_contract` has separate stable identity, provider and covered asset/period. | Registration changes and retirement are append-only revisions/events. Warranty terms have immutable versions; a claim is a separate event/case, not a boolean on asset. | Stress/readiness claims cite asset revision plus accepted evidence. Warranty eligibility cites exact contract version, event date and supporting work/evidence; a rating or modelled stress alone cannot prove claimability. | Define serial replacement versus same asset, ownership transfer, cover exclusions, vendor identity and monetary valuation basis. |
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:203:| Site onboarding | `site_draft` has its own UUID and tenant-scoped proposed code, separate from published `site.id`. `site_template` reuses the shipped catalog only as an explicitly versioned input; `site_import_batch` has stable upload identity and per-row disposition. | Draft revisions and setup events are append-only. Publish atomically allocates/resolves the Site code, initial Foundation and source registration; a failed batch row leaves no partial Site. Template updates never rewrite published Sites. | Persist source digest/template version and actor for every published Site; link import-row receipt to resulting Site/Foundation IDs. Imported physical/evidence claims still need gateway registration and accepted envelopes. | Define draft expiration, duplicate-code arbitration, template migration, batch atomicity/per-row retry and tenant authorization. The gated Lab create route is not the product onboarding contract. |
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:209:**Demo data dependency.** One Kobo window cannot substantiate the multi-site, multi-window, cold-chain or EV mock claims. Before their packets, define a small seed manifest per Site/window with source mode, Foundation/binding versions, gateway fixture, accepted/rejected evidence, analytic version, assumptions and expected claim limits. Rehearse both a complete and an Unknown/contradictory case through normal ingestion; do not stage Findings or prices directly. Portfolio requires comparable periods, currency, coverage and overlap rules across the seeded Sites.

"""What a model can do, as a table of executable handlers rather than a claim.

A model profile in `assetops_backend.runs.profiles` DECLARES which world states
a build can model and in which roles. Until T021 nothing could contradict it:
the backlog records that `supported_states` "has no falsifier", so `READY` was
computed from a promise about a kernel that did not exist.

This module is the other side of that. A `ModelSpec` holds one `StateHandler`
per `(state_key, role)` the model can actually consume, each carrying the
callable that consumes it, and `advertised_supported_states` is **derived by
grouping the handler table** rather than written beside it. Deleting a handler
narrows the advertised set; adding an advertised pair without a handler is
unspellable, because there is no field to write one in.

That makes the conformance test in `host/tests` a falsifier rather than a
second declaration: it compares the derived set against the product profile's
own, and it then executes a real frozen run and asserts every advertised pair
was reached. A table that merely agreed with another table would be two
declarations; a table whose every entry is called during an execution is a
claim with evidence under it.

## Two things a handler deliberately holds

`kind` says what sort of thing the handler consumes, because the five are
consumed at different phases and a reader needs to know which: a `STOCK` is
initialized and moved, a `BOUND_SOURCE` supplies a limit, a `COEFFICIENT` is
read by a law, a `FORCING` is read by a law only in the steps its window
concerns, and a `STATE_SAMPLE` answers what the state is at an instant.

A `STOCK` handler also carries its own `floor`. That is
`D-2026-09-22-reconciliation-panel-retirement`'s instruction to this slice: the
kernel takes its floor from the model, never from the validation layer's
`IMPLICIT_LOWER_BOUND_DIMENSIONS`. *Volume is non-negative* is a model rule, so
the model says it, and the simulator cannot reach that constant anyway because
it may not import the backend at all.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
from typing import Callable

from assetops_contracts.control import (
    AcceptedFlowSet,
    ControlIntent,
    DISCRETE_UNIT,
    ControllerView,
)
from assetops_contracts.trajectory import ForcingExposure
from assetops_contracts.world_inputs import BusAttachmentInput, SiteControlInput

#: The kinds of thing a handler consumes. Five, each consumed at a different
#: phase of the boundary cycle.
HANDLER_KINDS = frozenset(
    {"STOCK", "BOUND_SOURCE", "COEFFICIENT", "FORCING", "STATE_SAMPLE"}
)

#: This kernel's own version, part of every trajectory's identity. A kernel
#: change that could move a number moves this, so a golden trace bound to the
#: old number refuses playback rather than being reinterpreted.
#:
#: T025a added discrete state without moving it, deliberately: nothing it adds
#: is reached by a model that declares no discrete state, and the contract-9
#: merge gate (`host/tests/contract9_gate`) proves every pre-change fuel and
#: daytime Draft reproduces its identity, trajectory and observations exactly.
KERNEL_VERSION = 1


# --- Discrete state (T025a, v4 section 4.3) ----------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscreteStateSpec:
    """One addressed discrete state a model carries, declared rather than implied.

    - `allowed_values` is the closed set of members; a value outside it is a
      failure, never coerced.
    - `initialization_owner` says who answers the value at run start
      (`SCENARIO` for physical state, v4 section 10), and
      `initial_values_supported` which members this build can start from:
      one that needs timer history nobody can declare yet is refused rather
      than started with an invented history.
    - `transitions` is every permitted `(previous, next)` pair. The kernel
      refuses any other, so a pack cannot move a state along an edge the spec
      does not declare.
    - `observable_signals` names what the state is observable through; a
      physical state is not a reported reading until one of them is published.
    """

    state_key: str
    scope: str
    allowed_values: tuple[str, ...]
    initialization_owner: str
    initial_values_supported: frozenset[str]
    transitions: frozenset[tuple[str, str]]
    observable_signals: tuple[str, ...]
    statement: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        members = set(self.allowed_values)
        unknown = {
            value
            for pair in self.transitions
            for value in pair
            if value not in members
        } | (set(self.initial_values_supported) - members)
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Discrete state {self.state_key!r} names {sorted(unknown)}, "
"""The `mini-grid` v1 model: the daytime world plus a generator and its fuel.

T025a, under execution contract 9 and additively
(`D-2026-09-29-t025-generator-policy-package`). `daytime-mini-grid` v1 and
the fuel model are untouched; this pack reuses the daytime pack's PV,
battery, reserve and load machinery and adds:

- an addressed discrete state `generator-state` with the declared members
  STOPPED, STARTING, RUNNING and FAILED. This build starts from STOPPED or
  FAILED only - RUNNING and STARTING need timer history no scenario can yet
  declare - and moves only along STOPPED -> STARTING -> RUNNING -> STOPPED.
  FAILED is inert: no output, no transition in or out, and not an execution
  failure. Failure and recovery causes are T035's.
- **Site-owned control policy, and no trigger of the model's own.** The
  controller requests RUN only when the state of charge at T is at or below
  the Site's frozen `start-soc-threshold`, and STOP only when the generator
  is RUNNING, the Site's `minimum-runtime` has elapsed since it became
  RUNNING and the state of charge is at or above `stop-soc-threshold`.
- **The Site's frozen source priority, unsubstituted.** Sources serve load in
  that order (Kobo: PV, battery, generator), so a running generator serves
  what the sources ahead of it do not. It may also charge the battery, only
  when the battery is not discharging, within the PCS charge limit and the
  battery's headroom; the rating is a ceiling, never a command.
- **Fuel is checked before energy is accepted**, and falls by the Foundation's
  specific fuel consumption times accepted generator energy - to load and to
  the battery alike. The law is linear: no idle fuel and no start fuel, a
  simplification this pack discloses in every step's notes.

Lifecycle timing: transitions happen at step boundaries only. A start delay
that has elapsed is due in phase A, before the sample at T; a request emitted
at T moves the state in phase F, after the sample and the view. Start delay
and minimum runtime must be step-aligned: one ending inside a step is refused
rather than rounded.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from fractions import Fraction

from assetops_contracts.control import (
    DISCRETE_UNIT,
    AcceptedControl,
    AcceptedFlow,
    AcceptedFlowSet,
    BalanceIdentity,
    ControlIntent,
    ControlRequest,
    ControllerView,
    EffectiveControl,
    LoadService,
    StockAccount,
    ViewEntry,
)
from assetops_simulator.kernel.model import (
    ComponentRelation,
    Coupling,
    CouplingRefused,
    DiscreteStateSpec,
    ModelSpec,
    StateHandler,
    StepContext,
    TransitionRequest,
)
from assetops_simulator.packs import minigrid as daytime

GENERATOR_STATE = "generator-state"
GENERATOR_RATED_POWER = "generator-rated-power"
START_THRESHOLD = "generator-start-soc-threshold"
STOP_THRESHOLD = "generator-stop-soc-threshold"
MINIMUM_RUNTIME = "generator-minimum-runtime"

RUN, STOP = "RUN", "STOP"
HUNDRED = daytime.HUNDRED

    "fuel follows accepted generator energy at the Foundation's specific "

)

GENERATOR_LIFECYCLE = DiscreteStateSpec(
    state_key=GENERATOR_STATE,
    scope="COMPONENT",
    allowed_values=(STOPPED, STARTING, RUNNING, FAILED),
    initialization_owner="SCENARIO",
    initial_values_supported=frozenset({STOPPED, FAILED}),
    transitions=frozenset(
        {(STOPPED, STARTING), (STARTING, RUNNING), (RUNNING, STOPPED)}
    ),
    observable_signals=("generator-status (reported status is T025b's)",),
"""Generated device observations to canonical source envelopes.

What this is handed is the whole of what it can see: the reading series the
observation transform produced, the frozen mapping, the run's interval, and the
three publication identities the Draft froze. No world state, no trajectory, no
projection and no scenario - so there is no private value this function could
copy into an envelope even by mistake. It reads a `DeviceObservation`, which by
its own construction carries no true value.

## What is published, and what is not

- a `REPORTED` sample on an allowlisted signal is a `GOOD` envelope carrying the
  reported value - bias included, because that is what the device said;
- a sample that was TAKEN and had nothing to read - no completed interval, or no
  measurement - is an `UNAVAILABLE` envelope with a closed reason, and only on a
  signal whose mapping says it publishes unavailability;
- a sample that was never taken (`SUPPRESSED_BY_GAP`) or never arrived
  (`DROPPED`) publishes nothing. There is no envelope for a message that did not
  exist, and in particular no value is invented to fill the gap;
- a signal not on the allowlist publishes nothing at all.

The sentence that explains a suppression names the scenario's event, and it is
never copied: an envelope has no field it could go in.

## Identity and order

The message id is derived from the publication identity (`envelope_message_id`),
so republishing the same series yields the same ids - a retry is recognisable as
one. `sequence` counts one signal's publications from one, in source-time order,
so it too is a function of the series and not of how often it was published.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from assetops_contracts.observation import (
    DeviceObservation,
    HeldPublication,
    PublicationDelayWindow,
    publication_delay_minutes,
)
from assetops_contracts.operational_records import RecordObservation
from assetops_contracts.source_envelope import (
    EnvelopeRejected,
    SourceEnvelope,
    SourceMappingSet,
    envelope_message_id,
    instant_text,
    refuse_an_enum_under_schema_1,
)
from assetops_contracts.world_inputs import FrozenInterval

#: The transform's reading classes, in wire terms.
MEASUREMENT_OF_READING_CLASS = {
    "STATE_SIGNAL": "STATE_AT_INSTANT",
    "INTERVAL_SIGNAL": "INTERVAL_MEAN",
}

#: Sample outcomes that published nothing, because nothing was published.
NOT_PUBLISHED = frozenset({"SUPPRESSED_BY_GAP", "DROPPED"})

#: Sample outcomes that published a typed absence, and the closed reason each is.
UNAVAILABLE_REASON_OF_OUTCOME = {
    "NO_PRECEDING_INTERVAL": "NO_COMPLETED_INTERVAL",
    "NO_VALUE_AT_BOUNDARY": "NO_MEASUREMENT",
}


def _parse(instant: str) -> datetime:
    # Read by slice: the transform writes this one spelling, and `strptime` was
    # a large share of a stage write at the 8 h window (T026).
    return datetime(
        int(instant[0:4]),
        int(instant[5:7]),
        int(instant[8:10]),
        int(instant[11:13]),
        int(instant[14:16]),
        int(instant[17:19]),
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131:The existing v1/v2 envelope and v1 batch parser remain read-compatible for stored demo history. V3 **changes identity semantics**: `message_id = SHA256(length-delimited(site_id,gateway_id,source_id,sequence_epoch,sequence))`, with the tuple durably allocated once. Exact byte framing and test vectors are part of the contract task; do not concatenate without separators or trust a sender-supplied digest. Sequence scope is `(gateway,source,epoch)`; monotonic arrival is not required. `published_at` is when gateway seals publication, not a second measurement time. Source clock uncertainty is expressed as quality/diagnostic metadata, never correction of `source_time`. Batch V2 is homogeneous in source/mapping/Foundation reference; each envelope is still checked against effective configuration at its own time. The batch hash is over canonical bytes excluding the `payload_sha256` field; the receipt hash similarly excludes `receipt_sha256`. Batch `source_mode` is compared with authenticated gateway registration and is only provenance after that check. Wire IDs for site/gateway/source resolve registered UUID rows; device/signal IDs remain scoped source strings. A production batch must not carry Lab-private cause or truth; keep any Lab run reference in a private manifest keyed by batch ID.

# Decisions

## 2026-10-17

Decision: `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail`. Codex Architect applies the owner's full-v69-menu choice as a narrow amendment to the 2026-09-13 navigation rule and `D-2026-10-02-grammar-ownership-test`. The operator rail shows all eleven roots in six v69 groups, headed `OPERATIONS`, `INTELLIGENCE`, `WORK`, `ASSURANCE`, `BUSINESS`, `ADMIN`; only Sites (`/sites`) and Findings (`/findings`) are live W1 links. Other roots are visibly and accessibly `Not available yet` text with no destination or keyboard action; site-scoped pages do not make a cross-site root live. The Simulator Lab workspace-footer link appears only with its registered gated route, and unsupported demo-workspace/count copy is omitted. The navigation guard and route/component tests must prove the full inventory, absent destinations, real live routes, gate behavior, and 390 px access while retaining the separate Site tab/subtab bans. Exact contract: `.agent/architect-W1-UX-07-answers.md`. This decision does not promote panels or record owner acceptance.

Decision: `D-2026-10-17-w1-ux-06`. Codex Architect accepts W1-UX-06 questions 1-2: the S1 `READY_TO_REPORT` badge reads `Set up to report` with its configured tone and configuration-only meaning, and the Readiness strip reads `Set-up steps` because it has no contact read. Accept question 3 for current server contact with trusted, known old reported facts: the selected gateway state line says `In contact; its latest report is old`; unknown or untrusted fact age remains explicitly unconfirmed, and neither state claims equipment health. Amend question 4: unrecognised Readiness and Health codes get non-positive headlines, and zero enabled gateways never produce `All 0 enabled gateways`; preserve the recognised no-source reason and keep readiness-dependent steps from appearing complete on an unrecognised readiness code alone. Exact words, conditions and test expectations are in `.agent/architect-W1-UX-06-answers.md`. This refines `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`, `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health` and `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`; it neither promotes a panel nor records owner acceptance.

## 2026-10-16

Decision: `D-2026-10-16-s408`. Codex Architect accepts S4-08 Q1-Q3 with the display rule in `.agent/architect-S4-08-answers.md`. The Site consequence history includes unpriced versions with typed missing-price/null amount. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` identifies only the latest consequence within the latest FindingRevision recorded by that cutoff; if that FindingRevision has no consequence, no row for that Finding is current at cutoff. A selected past cutoff is audit history: an eligible recorded amount is labelled historical and assumption-conditioned, never a current-money claim. Exact-version source and existing money gates still govern display, and a closed source hides amounts. Only today's separate exact-head read may claim current money after all gates pass. This implements the Site-history requirement of `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons` without promoting `assumptions:3#a` or recording owner acceptance.

Decision: `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux-disclosure`. Codex Architect accepts the W1-UX-01 narrow disclosure scan as an amendment to `D-2026-10-04-plain-language-analysis-views`, with the exact banned disclosure-prose terms, permitted individually labelled references, and case-sensitive raw-code detection defined in `.agent/architect-W1-ux-disclosure-answers.md`. The closed disclosure is scanned, never exempted as a container; the existing single-value exemption rule stays. Raw IDs, versions, digests and reasons may appear only as labelled exact values, never in the collapsed summary. Raw-code matching does not flag ordinary units or acronyms including SOC, UTC, kW, kWh and PV. Plain state, material limits, scope and as-of time stay visible outside the disclosure. This does not record owner acceptance or change the W1 walkthrough gate.

Decision: `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`. Codex Architect. For W1 product surfaces, substantive deeper analysis has a separate contextual destination, following the owner-directed T028 Analysis tabs. Compact cards and indexes link to their owning detail; a closed inline "How this was worked out" disclosure holds only small exact-reference detail and is not the sole route to method or evidence. Keep the same destination at 1440 and 390 px. Approve Site, Evidence, Finding, Action, Verification and Analysis as explained domain/navigation labels; ban raw states, codes, IDs, implementation analytics and simulator vocabulary from the default reading, with narrowly labelled exact-reference exceptions in detail. Honest selected-read states and material limits take precedence over copy or mockup fidelity. Full screen-placement and vocabulary ruling: `.agent/architect-W1-ux-answers.md`. This amends the W1-UX-01 disclosure-only proposal and preserves `D-2026-10-04-plain-language-analysis-views`; owner acceptance remains the combined W1 walkthrough.

## 2026-10-07

Decision: `D-2026-10-07-s1-foundation-import-and-cutover`. Codex Architect, responding to the S1-01 handoff review under the delegated decision authority in `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md`. The controlling detail is [the S1-01 Foundation contract addendum](../frontend/docs/architecture/s1-01-foundation-contract-addendum.md). This records an Architect choice, not owner acceptance of S1-01 or a new owner sign-off on the three Site-first documents. Current project routing treats those documents as implementation authority; their proposal headers remain provenance until an owner acceptance record is identified.

S1 uses PostgreSQL 16 through pinned Docker Compose, psycopg 3, SQLAlchemy Core and Alembic. A separate migrated PostgreSQL test database is required. The first migration enables `btree_gist` and applies the current-valid Foundation exclusion constraint; publication also locks the Site row. No application-only overlap fallback is silently selected. Initial Foundation may exist before bindings, but no acquisition snapshot or evidence is authorized until S1-02 publishes bindings.

Import the Kobo fixture under a configured development tenant, preserving `KOBO-001`, its timezone and v1 source values. Register all ten physical/topological components as assets; retain device inventory in Foundation content. Legacy signal mappings are comparison/candidate data until S1-02 turns them into explicit role bindings. Register one pending simulated gateway and one source per fixture device; one source owns one device report stream and all its signals share the source sequence scope. Gateway credentials remain unprovisioned until the S2 boundary. Internal database IDs are UUIDs; product routes keep the tenant-scoped stable Site code.

Until authentication is built, the backend resolves a fixed configured development tenant; client-supplied tenant identity has no authority. S1 cuts over Foundation only for explicitly imported Sites. The existing YAML Site file is the expected source of that import, identified by path and digest, not a duplicate collision. A changed source or another same-code record conflicts. Product Site index/detail reads select Postgres for registered imported codes (one row) and retain YAML for unimported codes. Legacy product Overview, Evidence and dispatch must not display or write stale file-backed state for an imported code. The Lab currently shares the Site repository and YAML store; it has no separate Site namespace. From import, new Lab Draft setup and existing Draft start/step/run-to-end/Commit for that code are blocked until Lab uses the registered snapshot. The current Stage endpoint is a read-only inspector; it may show historical staged envelopes labelled with their frozen YAML basis but cannot generate or seal more. Commit is blocked even for a stage produced before import. Evidence, Performance and Overview cut over in their later named slices. S1 publishing is battery reserve SOC only; priority controls stay read-only. The Kobo fixture has a 30-minute generator minimum-runtime property but no 35%/90% generator thresholds. An incomplete Foundation gap fixture removes reserve while remaining well-formed under the existing strict Site parser. The Planner owns the API/UI/guard details and records the fixture comparison in the S1-01 packet.

+# Wave 2 architecture proposal — 2026-10-17
+
+**Status:** Architect design for Planner and owner scope confirmation; no W2 panel is thereby approved or delivered. W2 is the registry's 22 cold-chain/EV manifest IDs, Site first; W3 is estate aggregation, while domain health, work cases, recommendations, actions, cooling comparisons, availability and scenarios are W5 (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:274-277`). Existing ledger/panel/model mappings are candidates, not shipped claims (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-screen-ledger.json:1-5`; `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-panel-contracts.json:1-5`; `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-model-homes.json:1-5`).
+
+## Owner scope proposal
+
+Recommend **Real with honest gaps** for the IDs below. A listed core panel becomes real only after its stated record/claim gate; until then render an honest selected-window absence. Other panels may show supported portions and labelled gaps, with no mock values. Panel numbers/titles are from `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-panel-contracts.json:912-1000` and the corresponding W2 `screen_id` entries; the mock intent and adaptations are in `frontend/mockups/v69/screen-manifest.json:398-573,880-986,1057-1074,1100-1286,1485-1501,1571-1658` and `frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:390-451`.
+
+| Manifest ID | Core panel(s) recommended | Real claim / honest gap at first release |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `cold` | 1 What happened; 2 Chamber temperature | Evidence-backed outage → cooling → temperature story and chart; maintenance/action and site-wide status remain bounded or absent. |
+| `temperature` | 1 Temperature with operating context; 3 Claim boundary | Measured samples, policy band, interval bounds; no spoilage or precise interpolated duration. |
+| `coldbackup` | 1 What the chain shows; 4 Evidence by causal step | Observed start request/result, power and compressor order; missing transfer position is a missing link, never a proven switch state. |
+| `cold-foundation` | 1 Physical site; 2 Operating interpretation | Published effective Foundation and role bindings; undeclared equipment is unknown. |
+| `cold-evidence` | 1 What we can conclude now; 2 Data sources | Capability by claim plus accepted/absent/stale sources; no equipment-health inference from source health. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` | 1 Accepted records; 2 How a claim limit is set | Exact accepted IDs, times, quality and exclusions; no Lab truth. |
+| `cold-financials` | 1 What happened → money; 3 Not included | Unpriced product exposure and recorded repair cost only; no spoilage amount. |
+| `cold-assumptions` | 1 Financial assumptions | Versioned stock valuation/exposure-policy inputs, initially undeclared. |
+| `cold-finding` | 1 Bounded conclusion; 2 Operational context | Automatic-start failure Finding if chain is complete; diagnosis of failed part unknown. |
+| `cold-finding-excursion` | 1 Bounded conclusion; 2 Temperature | Distinct temperature Finding with duration bounds and same source lineage, no duplicate consequence. |
+| `cold-readiness` | 1 Readiness and commissioning evidence; 3 Connected sources | Configuration/readiness and current source contact separately; commissioning unconfirmed without record. |
+| `cold-sld` | 1 Diagram; 2 What each part does | Effective declared topology and mapped reporting links; undrawn protection/switches shown as undeclared. |
+| `ev` | 1 What happened on the service day; 2 Charging activity and site power | Canonical service/energy/site-power summaries; charger cause, action and condition unknown. |
+| `sessions` | 1 Session service records; 2 How to read the denominator; 3 Cause remains bounded | Completed, observed aborted, incomplete distinct; missing end is unknown. |
+| `ev-foundation` | 1 Physical site; 2 Operating interpretation | Effective chargers/connectors, site limit and bindings. |
+| `ev-evidence` | 1 What we can conclude now; 2 Data sources | Session/status/meter capability with missing-hour limitation. |
+| `ev-evidence-analysis` | 1 Accepted records; 2 How a claim limit is set | Exact session/connector/meter records and claim gates. |
+| `ev-financials` | 1 What happened → money; 3 Not included | Gross tariff **scenario estimate** for supported metered kWh only; collections and lost sales unknown. |
+| `ev-assumptions` | 1 Financial assumptions | Versioned scenario tariff/currency, explicitly distinct from billing evidence. |
+| `ev-finding` | 1 Bounded conclusion; 2 Operational context | Observed fault-aborted session, unknown fault cause and unmet demand. |
+| `ev-readiness` | 1 Readiness and commissioning evidence; 3 Connected sources | Configured connectors/limit and source contact; no implied commissioning or billing link. |
+| `ev-sld` | 1 Diagram; 2 What each part does | Declared supply, meter, chargers and connectors, with reporting status only where supported. |
+
+**Deliberate W2 gaps/deferment.** In `cold`/`ev`, panels 3 and 5 may link to *existing* Findings and evidence requests but must not imply domain Action, Incident, Maintenance, Recommendation or verified outcome; those are W5. `coldbackup` panel 3 may list only derived event chains, not an Incident record. No `cooling`, `availability`, `energy`, domain health, scenarios or estate root is silently promoted. `ev` site-power “Healthy” is limited to observed power versus declared limit, not site/charger health. The mock's `sessions` wording mixes “complete records” and the aborted session's 3.6 kWh in its 120 kWh (`frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:433-450`): sum only individually bounded meter intervals and label their session outcomes, or omit the 120/$36 example if that reconciliation fails. Cold charts use sampled bounds (25–35 minutes and bounded °C·min only if the accepted series reproduces them), not interpolation (`frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:399-404`).
+
+**Owner to confirm:** all 22 IDs versus a narrower cold-first release; the above core panel numbers and tier per ID; demonstrator targets **GreenHarvest** 2–4°C, five-minute probe, 6 Oct 19:00–20:00, grid/start/cooling disturbance, and **Depot** two 60 kW connectors, 80 kW site limit, 6 Oct 08:00–16:00, 12 attempts with 10 complete/1 observed abort/1 incomplete, 120 kWh and $0.30/kWh *scenario* tariff. These are fixture targets from `frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js:51-52,390-450`, not approved readings or seeded product rows. Recommend the IDs/tier above; allow actual deterministic accepted output to change displayed numbers rather than forcing the simulator to fit the mock.
+
+## Domain seams and claim rules
+
+Reuse tenant/Site identity, immutable time-valid Foundation and binding revisions, source registrations, typed `EvidenceQuery`, S2 AcceptedEvidence/receipt, selected half-open window and knowledge cutoff, revisioned Finding/financial consequence, S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance, W1 Site shell and separate Analysis destination (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-84`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:22-54,173-183`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-19,4165-4188`). Keep `COLDCHAIN` and `EV_CHARGING` as explicit site types; bind each physical source signal to asset, semantic role, unit, cadence, valid period and mapping/Foundation revision. A signal cannot fill two incompatible roles by label alone. Historical revisions remain inspectable; absent/stale/contradictory roles produce claim-specific Unknown. No status is inferred from gateway contact alone.
+
+| Contract | Cold-chain | EV charging |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| Foundation/roles | Chamber/probe and policy low/high °C required for excursion; compressor run/power, grid import/status, generator request/state, backup bus or inverter state required **per causal step**. Door, defrost, ambient, load and transfer position optional context whose absence blocks those explanations. Policy and calibration versioned; readings in UTC with Site-local display. | Connector identity and enabled schedule, session ID/start/end/outcome, interval meter kWh, connector status, site import kW and effective site/charger limits. Request/current limit and vehicle demand optional for power-constraint diagnosis; tariff/billing independent. Require declared status cadence and connector schedule for availability, which W2 does not promise. |
+| Evidence/assessment | Validated °C samples and policy yield crossing interval `[last in-band, first out-of-band]` and return interval; duration/°C·min lower/upper bounds, coverage, peak **observed** value. Missing probe segment cannot be bridged. Backup chain matches ordered accepted events within declared timestamp tolerance, reports each missing step and never infers starter-part fault from timing. Distinguish temperature excursion, observed failed automatic start, and unknown cause. | Correlate records by scoped charger/connector/session ID; duplicate/conflicting IDs quarantine. Start + end + accepted metered intervals establish delivered energy; observed fault/stop establishes abort; absent end remains incomplete. Summaries keep complete/aborted/incomplete denominators and known/unknown time. Below-limit site import is context, not proof that the site supply did not constrain a session without synchronized request/limit/voltage evidence. |
+| Finding/currentness | Separate excursion and start-response Finding identities/revisions, exact evidence IDs, configuration and model versions; both may cite the same event but no duplicated money. Late evidence queues recomputation and visibly marks previous revision stale until rebuilt. | Session-interruption Finding cites exact fault/stop/meter evidence; incomplete sessions are evidence gaps, not failures. Late end/status can supersede through a new revision; missing data never silently becomes normal. |
+| Money | Excursion → unpriced **potential product exposure** unless stock identity, quantity/value and a reviewed product-specific exposure-response rule are present; temperature alone never proves spoilage. Recorded call-out/repair expense needs an actual work/cost record. No summed loss across overlapping backup and excursion Findings. | Metered supported energy × versioned tariff gives gross *scenario* estimate, not collections, margin or lost revenue. Lost revenue requires credible counterfactual demand, price and service baseline; W2 keeps it unpriced. Warranty/repair scenarios wait for asset/work contracts. |
+| Readiness/SLD | Reuse S1 configuration readiness and S7 source health separately. Draw declared grid/PV/battery/control bus/generator/compressor/probe links; label inferred electrical-to-cooling relationship as configuration, not live flow. | Draw effective site feed/meter/charger/connector links and configured limits; no stationary battery unless Foundation declares one. SLD monitoring markers derive from bindings + source health, never drawing alone. |
+
+Both analytics consume only normalized AcceptedEvidence with quality, source/measurement/receive times, role and effective configuration. Output stores input digest (accepted IDs/content, mapping/Foundation, window and model version), bounded quantities, reasons and projection watermark. Clock skew, low cadence, gaps and contradiction can independently lower capability. Current-money reads retain LR and S4-05 source gates; `Generated test data` and `Prepared example data` labels are adjacent to material claim limits, mixed/unknown suppress current amounts (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:4165-4188`). W1 plain default with chart/timeline and contextual detail applies from each slice; no raw codes/IDs or simulator terms in the default reading (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-15`).
+
+## Simulator-to-evidence contract
+
+1. Add **versioned cold pack** to `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/`: thermal stock (chamber/ambient heat exchange and load), door-opening heat pulse, compressor on/off and cooling capacity, control power versus compressor power, grid-loss/backup transfer and generator start/failure. Compose with existing electrical mini-grid machinery only at explicit power ports; no event timeline written directly into reports. Declare discrete states/transitions and initialization; step alignment and conservation/temperature sanity guards are executable. Private world has disturbance cause, target trajectory and oracle; public device transform sees only probe, meter, controller and manual-log observations (`simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/model.py:1-110`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/mini_grid.py:1-100`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/publish.py:1-45`).
+2. Add **versioned EV pack**: charger/connector availability, requests, negotiated limit, site shared-power cap, session start/energy/end/fault state, meter energy integration. A vehicle-request schedule is private forcing, not accepted demand evidence. Device transform publishes charger events/status, interval meter and site meter separately. Simulated missing end/status is a publication-path loss, not a fabricated `FAILED` event.
+3. Extend `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` and registered Lab basis with exact pack/model version, Foundation values, role/source mapping, report cadence, observation bias/gaps, gateway publication schedule and frozen scenario digest; reject unsupported states before Draft. Add `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` and `ev-*.yaml` plus profile versions. Stage seals public envelopes; registered Commit binds run/batch before ordinary gateway release. Continue through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2, S2 receipt and AcceptedEvidence; no direct Finding or evidence seed and no private manifest in product bytes (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-75`; `host/gateway_publication.py:1-25`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
+4. One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).
+
+## Ordered slices (one branch and independent review each; at most two phases)
+
+| Slice / size | Observable exit; predecessor; schema and registry nodes |
+| --- | --- |
+| **W2-01 Cold Foundation and topology / S — Planner ready** | Phase 1: publish one `COLDCHAIN` Site Foundation, assets, time-valid role bindings/policy and gateway/source registrations with complete and missing-probe examples. Phase 2: `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld` render stored configuration and honest contact/commissioning gaps at 1440/390. Predecessor S1/S7/W1. Migration: site-type/asset-role/policy validation or additive typed configuration tables as needed; never overwrite S1 history. Nodes `A-cold-role-set`, `A-cold-site-projections` (config portion), `I-configuration-breadth-store`; propose `I-w2-cold-foundation-ui`, `D-w2-cold-foundation`. Acceptance: selected effective revision changes diagram/role gap, unknown contact remains unknown, no evidence claim. |
+| **W2-02 Cold causal producer / M** | Phase 1 pack/scenarios/profile; phase 2 frozen Lab Stage/Commit and ordinary gateway receipt, plus positive/negative/missing/late replay and vendor-format peer mapping. Depends W2-01, S2, registered Lab and outbox. Migration: profile/fixture registration and adapter mapping only if persisted schema needs it. Nodes `A-cold-world`, `I-cold-world`, `I-cold-device-adapter`, `D-cold-physical-peer`; propose `D-w2-cold-replay`. |
+| **W2-03 Cold assessment and Finding / M** | Phase 1 typed evidence, bounded excursion/start-chain rules, immutable assessment/Finding revisions and late recompute; phase 2 `temperature`, `coldbackup`, `cold-finding`, `cold-finding-excursion` with lineage and missing-state detail. Depends W2-02. Migration: domain assessment/episode tables + model/basis digest and recompute queue, reusing Finding store. Nodes `A-cold-excursion-rules`, `I-cold-assessment-store`, `D-cold-domain`; propose `I-w2-cold-assessment-ui`. |
+| **W2-04 Cold Site/evidence/money / M** | Phase 1 Site/Capability/Evidence projections and unpriced consequence/assumptions; phase 2 `cold`, `cold-evidence`, `cold-evidence-analysis`, `cold-financials`, `cold-assumptions` with connected drilldowns. Depends W2-03, S4-05. Migration: domain assumption keys and consequence basis where existing schema cannot represent bounded unpriced quantity; no direct amount seeding. Nodes `A-evidence-shared-projections`, `A-cold-site-projections`, `D-cold-domain`; propose `I-w2-cold-site-ui`, `I-w2-cold-money`. |
+| **W2-05 EV Foundation and producer / M** | Phase 1 `EV_CHARGING` Foundation/bindings/topology and versioned charger/session pack; phase 2 replay through Lab/gateway plus vendor-format peer, and `ev-foundation`, `ev-readiness`, `ev-sld` with honest gaps. Depends W2-01 shared configuration seam and S2. Migration: connector/session identity/configuration and profile rows; no mock hardcoding. Nodes `A-ev-role-set`, `A-ev-world`, `I-ev-world`, `I-ev-device-adapter`, `D-ev-physical-peer`; propose `I-w2-ev-foundation-ui`, `D-w2-ev-replay`. |
+| **W2-06 EV assessment and Finding / M** | Phase 1 session correlation, supported meter sum, interruption/unknown states, Finding and late recompute; phase 2 `sessions`, `ev-finding` with exact accepted record links. Depends W2-05. Migration: session/connector windows and immutable assessment revision; reuse Finding store. Nodes `A-ev-session-rules`, `I-ev-assessment-store`, `D-ev-domain`; propose `I-w2-ev-assessment-ui`. |
+| **W2-07 EV Site/evidence/money / M** | Phase 1 shared projections and tariff scenario consequence with exact source/price gates; phase 2 `ev`, `ev-evidence`, `ev-evidence-analysis`, `ev-financials`, `ev-assumptions`, preserving incomplete sessions and unpriced loss. Depends W2-06, S4-05, W2-04 projection pattern. Migration: tariff assumption key and consequence quantity/basis if needed. Nodes `A-ev-site-projections`, `D-ev-domain`; propose `I-w2-ev-site-ui`, `I-w2-ev-money`. |
+
+Each UI phase carries v69 content/placement review, 1440/390 behavior, plain-language vocabulary scan and genuine stored replay evidence; no end-of-wave UX patch. W2-01 Planner should specify exact Foundation schema delta, registration roles, missing-probe fixture, selected-read API, UI destinations, migration/rollback and acceptance tests before implementation. Existing registry nodes are left open until reviewed; proposed nodes above are **not inserted**, so no checker-triggering speculative status change.
+

+
+1. **Scope/tier:** approve all 22 as Real with honest gaps, or choose cold-first subset. Recommend all 22 as W2 target, delivered in the ordered cold-then-EV slices; W5 work/health stays deferred.

One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).
+
+## Ordered slices (one branch and independent review each; at most two phases)
+
+| Slice / size | Observable exit; predecessor; schema and registry nodes |
+| --- | --- |

Migration: site-type/asset-role/policy validation or additive typed configuration tables as needed; never overwrite S1 history. Nodes `A-cold-role-set`, `A-cold-site-projections` (config portion), `I-configuration-breadth-store`; propose `I-w2-cold-foundation-ui`, `D-w2-cold-foundation`. W2-01 Planner should specify exact Foundation schema delta, registration roles, missing-probe fixture, selected-read API, UI destinations, migration/rollback and acceptance tests before implementation.
